# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-12)

## Corpus Check
- 8968 files · ~21,666,863 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 668 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .log 519, (none) 43, .css 18)

## Summary
- 86710 nodes · 251935 edges · 2363 communities (1669 shown, 694 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 11099 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `13313f03`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
